def have_login(today=None):
    from data_acquisition import kite
    try:
        kite.load_token(today=today); return True
    except Exception:
        return False

def run_market_loop(max_minutes=None, today=None, _sleep=None):
    import time
    _sleep = _sleep or time.sleep
    n = 0
    from scripts.recorder_manifest import session_start
    session_start(STORE, "E13 five-level depth", logged_in=have_login(today=today), today=today)   # R-FIX-3E (4)
    while have_login(today=today):
        now = datetime.now(IST); hm = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
        if now.weekday() >= 5 or not (9*60+15 <= hm <= 15*60+30):
            break
        record_once(today=today); n += 1
        if max_minutes and n >= max_minutes:
            break
        _sleep(60)
    return n

    q = {"NSE:X": {"depth": {"buy": [{"price": 100, "quantity": 5}] * 5, "sell": [{"price": 101, "quantity": 4}] * 5}}}
    rows = _rows_from_quote(q)

C. CLAUDE PRICE ENGINE — ARCHITECTURE (LEAD-RULED, fixed; contents come from backtests)
C0 Identity: engine 2 of 8; own folder under the engine bus, own state, own paper ledger, own Telegram line "Claude Price Engine". Same pipeline as the [redacted] Price Engine — bars → rulers → patterns → measured record → sizing → queue → paper ledger — only the ruler shelf differs. Reads shared stores READ-ONLY; never touches the [redacted] Price Engine's rulers, queue or ledger. Absorbs Insurance (portfolio protection) and Context (regime) as chunks 5 and 6 — they are not separate engines.
C1 Money math (same functions as the Price Engine; cite each in the doc): fees COST_STATUTORY_RT + cost_readout (money.py:17, :189); fill bar + six checks liquidity_backtest.checklist (:185) + paper.simulate_trade; sizing engine_book.weight (Sizing Families) with the adaptive multiplier 0→1 on Kelly computed from the pattern's measured record — never a fixed fraction; a pattern trades flat until n_min (the existing constant, cite — do not type a new one); starting capital NOTIONAL_CAPITAL (money.py:13); caps and exits from the ruler's measured profile (chunk 3); failed rulers → probation via diary_engines.
C2 No typed numbers: every threshold is a percentile of a measured distribution on the training window, chosen by walk-forward backtest; the doc records percentile + window; the code reads them from the ruler's record file, never from a literal.
CHUNK 1 — Data + context layer: point-in-time universe (build item 8 here, shared read-only), daily bars, index bars, realised-volatility and breadth series from those bars, and the expiry/holiday/event calendar (the Calendar engine's file). Output: one context row per trading day, with a leakage test proving no row uses data after its own date. DONE = tests GREEN + the context file regenerates from the stores.
CHUNK 2 — Direction rulers (the shelf): candidate families, each must earn its place in walk-forward with cost_readout applied — trend continuation (close vs rolling reference), mean reversion (distance from reference in ATR units), range breakout, gap continuation vs gap fade at the open, volume expansion, relative strength vs the index. Index close-to-open is NOT here (it lives in the [redacted] Price Engine). A family enters the shelf only if its out-of-sample record clears the six checks with positive expectancy after costs at n ≥ n_min; else probation. """R-AUTO-CONTINUE: a Stop hook so the builder never stops on its own while checklist items remain.

It BLOCKS the stop and re-issues "continue in order" when ALL of these hold:
  - the journal's next item is non-empty (reports/BUILD-STATE.json "next"),
  - no HALT exists (E1-DESK\\HALT),
  - the last self-flag is exactly "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD",
  - it is outside the 08:30–15:45 build pause (desk_clock.building()),
  - the checklist still has remaining items (reports/CHECKLIST.json).
Otherwise it lets the session stop — i.e. it stops for LEAD RULING NEEDED, a HALT, the checklist finished, or
the pause window. Fail-open: any error lets the stop proceed (never trap the session on a hook bug).
"""
import json
import os
import sys

REPO = os.environ.get("E1_REPO") or r"[redacted]\Desktop\trading-engines-v2"
STATE = os.environ.get("E1_STATE") or os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-STATE.json")
CHECKLIST = os.environ.get("E1_CHECKLIST") or os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CHECKLIST.json")
HALT = os.environ.get("E1_HALT_FILE") or os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "HALT")


def _load(p):
    try:
        return json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _building():
    try:
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import desk_clock
        return desk_clock.building()
    except Exception:
        from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
        now = datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))
        m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
        return m >= 15 * 60 + 45 or m < 8 * 60 + 30      # fail toward the build window (keep going)


def should_continue():
    """Continue while the checklist has work — and, per R-BACKLOG, when it empties, pull the next backlog item
    into the checklist and keep going. Stop only for a HALT, a LEAD RULING NEEDED flag, the pause window, or an
    empty checklist AND empty backlog."""
    st = _load(STATE)
    flag = (st.get("flag") or "").strip()
    if os.path.exists(HALT) or flag != "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD" or not _building():
        return False
    remaining = [x for x in (_load(CHECKLIST).get("remaining") or []) if x]
    if remaining:
        return True
    try:                                              # checklist empty -> pull the next backlog item (R-BACKLOG)
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import backlog
        return bool(backlog.pull_next())
    except Exception:
        return False


def main():
    try:
        raw = sys.stdin.read()
    except Exception:
        raw = ""
    try:
        if should_continue():
            reason = "continue in order"
            try:                                   # R-VERIFIER v2 gate: verify the last commit before the next item
                sys.path.insert(0, REPO)           # ADVISORY until the owner arms E1-DESK\\VERIFIER-ARMED
                from scripts import verifier
                g = verifier.gate_reason()
                if g:
                    reason = g
            except Exception:
                pass
            # block the stop and hand the session the same instruction the owner would type
            print(json.dumps({"decision": "block", "reason": reason}))
    except Exception:
        pass
    sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== run_evening.py (main) ===
"""Armed EOD evening runner (owner dispatch E1-PAPER-1 step 5). Detached: waits until after
15:30 IST today, re-fetches today's complete 1-minute bars (token already on disk — NO new login),
merges them, runs the evening cycle (daily-horizon signals → three lights → queue for next open),
regenerates the dashboard, sends the [E1] evening ping, and sends the dashboard as a Telegram
document. Paper only; no broker order calls. If the Kite API reports credits/subscription/payment,
it stops, spends nothing, and surfaces the exact message as a 🙋.
"""
import os, sys, time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from scripts.notify import notify, send_document
from engine1_rebuild import evening, dashboard, trading_calendar as CAL


def _ist():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))


def main():
    now = _ist()
    close = now.replace(hour=15, minute=35, second=0, microsecond=0)
    trading_today, note = CAL.is_trading_day_or_weekday(now.date())
    if note:
        notify(f"[E1] {note}")
    if not trading_today or close <= now:               # the exchange calendar, holidays included
        try:
            nxt = CAL.next_session(now.date())
        except Exception:
            nxt = now.date() + timedelta(days=1)
            while nxt.weekday() >= 5:
                nxt += timedelta(days=1)
        close = close.replace(year=nxt.year, month=nxt.month, day=nxt.day)
    wait = (close - now).total_seconds()
    if wait > 60:
        notify(f"[E1] EOD evening run armed for {close.strftime('%a %d %b %H:%M')} IST "
               f"(~{int(wait/3600)}h away). That day's daily-horizon signals queue for the next open.")
        while _ist() < close:                          # sleep in chunks so a long wait is restartable
            time.sleep(min((close - _ist()).total_seconds(), 3600))
    # re-fetch today's complete minute bars so today's daily bar is whole, then merge (reuse top-up merger)
    try:
        from data_acquisition import kite
        from scripts import kite_login
        today = _ist().date().isoformat()
        kite.fetch_slices([(str(t), "minute", today, today) for t in __import__(
            "engine1_rebuild.datastore", fromlist=["frozen_tokens"]).frozen_tokens()])
        kite_login._merge_topup_into_store(today)
    except Exception as e:
        notify(f"\U0001f64b [E1] EOD could not refresh today's bars ({type(e).__name__}) — running on "
               f"the data already on disk. If this is a Kite credits/subscription message, nothing was "
               f"paid: {str(e)[:120]}")
    # advance existing daily paper trades first (fills at the next open, exits, marks), then new signals
    try:
        pos = evening.process_eod_positions()
    except Exception as e:
        pos = {"filled": 0, "closed": 0, "open": 0}
        notify(f"[E1] EOD position update skipped ({type(e).__name__}); new signals still run.")

"""R-DAY1-BUNDLE A4: E10 and E11 as ENGINES. Each is a REAL ₹2,00,000 paper book written from the SAME
E1 fires as the flat book, but sized by its OWN rule and kept on its OWN account:

    E10  — the FACTS/WEIGHTS route: risk = weight(family) × ₹1,000, weight from the armed e10 shadow
    E11  — the NORMAL family bias:  risk = normal_weight(family) × ₹1,000, from the armed e11 shadow

(E11's INVERSE / season / expiry variants stay diaries inside E11 — only NORMAL sizes a paper order here.)

The order path stays the flat book's (same locks, fills, ~20 bps costs, exits, 20-position cap — nothing
here forks the fill code); this module only RECORDS what each engine's sizing would have made in its own
ledger, so E1/E10/E11 can be compared live. Each fire books at weight × the row's own risk; each exit books
result_R × THAT row's risk (never ×₹1,000), so an engine's equity diverges from flat exactly as its sizing
differs. COLD START = FLAT: a family with no measured weight (or an unreadable shadow) sizes at 1.0 — never
larger on a number the engine lacks, the same rule adaptive_book uses. Each open/exit also appends a ROOF
event tagged with the engine, so the roof copies each engine's EXECUTED decision. Nothing here places an
order; under the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold no fire fills, so these ledgers stay empty until the book trades.
RECORD-ONLY.
"""

"""NB41 item 1 — the 8 non-chain engine wrappers on DiaryEngine (CLAUDE.md §27 b-d gates INHERITED).

=== diary_engine.py ===
"""NB40 item 2 (foundation) — the DIARY-ONLY ENGINE BASE, with CLAUDE.md §27 (b)-(d) gates baked in.

Every non-chain engine runs as its own process THROUGH this base, so §27 isolation is enforced by construction,
not by each engine remembering it:
  (b) DIARY-ONLY — record() writes only the engine's own diary; there is NO order path here, ever.
  (c) STARTS LATE, RAM-GATED — should_start() is True only AFTER the 08:15 READY line AND when measured free RAM
      >= the reopen floor + this engine's OWN measured footprint (both passed in — measured, no typed number, §4.1).
  (d) QUIET — a fault is caught and written to the engine's diary/held log; it NEVER posts a 🙋 (only a ✅/⏱ line).
  killed-first — PRIORITY marks these processes so the watchdog kills them BEFORE the chain (the chain is never
      killed for another engine).

The engine's work is INJECTED (`work_fn`), so the base is unit-tested with a fake and spends nothing; each of the
8 engine items becomes a thin wrapper: `DiaryEngine("E-x", footprint_gb=<measured>, work_fn=...)`. BUILT + tested;
an engine is LIVE only after its OWN rule-vs-live audit + smoke check, switched on in the after-close window.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as _dt
import os

_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
DIARY_DIR = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "diaries")
PRIORITY = "diary"          # killed-first marker (§27c): the watchdog kills PRIORITY=="diary" processes before the chain


def _reopen_floor():
    try:
        from scripts import session_resurrect
        return session_resurrect.REOPEN_FLOOR_GB
    except Exception:
        return 0.72          # the measured reopen floor (session_resurrect.REOPEN_FLOOR_GB); fallback only


def _footprint_path(name):
    return os.path.join(DIARY_DIR, "%s.footprint" % name)


def measured_footprint(name):
    """This engine's OWN measured RAM footprint in GB, recorded on a first run — or None if not yet calibrated
    (fail-closed: an uncalibrated engine does NOT start; §4.1 no typed footprint)."""
    try:
        return float(open(_footprint_path(name), encoding="utf-8").read().strip())
    except Exception:
        return None


def record_footprint(name, gb=None):
    """Record this engine's measured footprint. gb=None measures THIS process's peak working set now (calibration
    on first run). Returns the GB recorded."""
    if gb is None:
        gb = _peak_ws_gb()
    if gb is None:
        return None
    try:
        os.makedirs(DIARY_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        open(_footprint_path(name), "w", encoding="utf-8").write("%.3f" % float(gb))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return round(float(gb), 3)


def _peak_ws_gb():
    """Peak working set of THIS process in GB (Windows), or None — the measured footprint on a first run."""
    try:
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes

        class _PMC(ctypes.Structure):
            _fields_ = [("cb", wintypes.DWORD), ("PageFaultCount", wintypes.DWORD),
                        ("PeakWorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t), ("WorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("QuotaPeakPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("QuotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("PagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("PeakPagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t)]
        pmc = _PMC(); pmc.cb = ctypes.sizeof(_PMC)
        k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        k32.GetCurrentProcess.restype = wintypes.HANDLE     # pointer-sized handle (not truncated to c_int on 64-bit)
        # K32GetProcessMemoryInfo (kernel32) is the canonical export; psapi's forwarded GetProcessMemoryInfo
        # returns 0 (fails) on this box. Declaring argtypes is load-bearing: without it ctypes passes the 64-bit
        # process handle as a 32-bit int and the call fails (0), which left the §27c footprint gate permanently
        # uncalibrated (no diary engine could ever start).
        fn = getattr(k32, "K32GetProcessMemoryInfo", None) or ctypes.windll.psapi.GetProcessMemoryInfo
        fn.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, ctypes.POINTER(_PMC), wintypes.DWORD]
        fn.restype = wintypes.BOOL
        if fn(k32.GetCurrentProcess(), ctypes.byref(pmc), pmc.cb):
            return round(pmc.PeakWorkingSetSize / (1024 ** 3), 3)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def should_start(ready_seen, free_gb, footprint_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
    """§27c: a non-chain engine starts ONLY after the 08:15 READY line AND with free RAM at/above the reopen floor
    PLUS this engine's own measured footprint. Fail-closed: unknown/None RAM or footprint -> do not start."""
    if not ready_seen:
        return False
    floor = _reopen_floor() if reopen_floor_gb is None else reopen_floor_gb
    if free_gb is None or footprint_gb is None:
        return False
    return float(free_gb) >= float(floor) + float(footprint_gb)


class DiaryEngine:
    """A non-chain engine: diary-only, RAM-gated, quiet, killed-first (§27 b-d). Orders are impossible here."""

    priority = PRIORITY

    def __init__(self, name, footprint_gb, work_fn, diary_fn=None, clock_fn=None):
        self.name = name
        self.footprint_gb = footprint_gb          # this engine's OWN measured RAM footprint (§27c)
        self.work_fn = work_fn                     # the engine's diary work; returns a short status string
        self.diary_fn = diary_fn or self._append_diary
        self.clock_fn = clock_fn or (lambda: _dt.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"))

    def _append_diary(self, line):
        try:
            os.makedirs(DIARY_DIR, exist_ok=True)
            with open(os.path.join(DIARY_DIR, "%s.log" % self.name), "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(line + "\n")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def run_once(self, ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
        """Run one diary cycle IF the §27c gate allows. Returns {started, status|reason}. A fault is caught and
        written to the diary as a quiet ⏱ line — NEVER a 🙋 (§27d)."""
        fp = self.footprint_gb if self.footprint_gb is not None else measured_footprint(self.name)
        if not should_start(ready_seen, free_gb, fp, reopen_floor_gb):
            return {"started": False, "reason": "gate: ready=%s free=%s footprint=%s (None=uncalibrated)" % (
                ready_seen, free_gb, fp)}
        try:
            status = self.work_fn()               # diary-only work; no order path exists to reach
            line = "✅ %s %s — %s" % (self.name, self.clock_fn(), status)
        except Exception as e:                     # §27d: quiet — the fault goes to the diary, no owner ping
            line = "⏱ %s %s — fault %s (diary-only, not an owner ping)" % (self.name, self.clock_fn(), type(e).__name__)
            self.diary_fn(line)
            return {"started": True, "status": "fault", "line": line}
        self.diary_fn(line)
        return {"started": True, "status": "ok", "line": line}


def _demo():
    floor = 0.72
    # §27c gate: needs ready + free RAM >= floor + footprint
    assert should_start(True, floor + 0.3, 0.2, floor) is True          # 1.02 >= 0.72+0.2
    assert should_start(True, floor + 0.1, 0.2, floor) is False         # 0.82 < 0.92 -> hold
    assert should_start(False, 9.9, 0.2, floor) is False                # not ready -> hold (before 08:15)
    assert should_start(True, None, 0.2, floor) is False                # unknown RAM -> fail-closed
    posts = []
    eng = DiaryEngine("E-demo", footprint_gb=0.2, work_fn=lambda: "12 evaluated, 3 fired", diary_fn=posts.append,
                      clock_fn=lambda: "10:00:00")
    # gated off before READY
    assert eng.run_once(False, 9.9)["started"] is False and posts == []
    # runs after READY with RAM -> one ✅ diary line, no ping
    r = eng.run_once(True, floor + 0.5)
    assert r["started"] and r["status"] == "ok" and posts[-1].startswith("✅ E-demo") and "fired" in posts[-1]
    # a faulting engine stays quiet: a ⏱ diary line, never a 🙋
    bad = DiaryEngine("E-bad", 0.1, work_fn=lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(RuntimeError("boom")), diary_fn=posts.append,
                      clock_fn=lambda: "10:01:00")
    rb = bad.run_once(True, floor + 0.5)
    assert rb["status"] == "fault" and posts[-1].startswith("⏱ E-bad") and "🙋" not in posts[-1]
    print("diary_engine demo OK: §27c RAM+READY gate; diary-only ✅ line; faults quiet (⏱, never 🙋); killed-first marker.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
        _demo()

ENGINES = {name: _make(name, desc, source) for (name, desc, source) in _SPECS}


def run_once(name, ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
    """Run one diary cycle for one engine through its §27 gate. Unknown name -> None."""
    eng = ENGINES.get(name)
    return eng.run_once(ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb) if eng else None


def names():
    return sorted(ENGINES)


def calibrate(name):
    """NB48 item 3 — the FIRST supervised calibration run for one diary engine (§27 enabler): run its diary work
    ONCE, write its first diary line, and record THIS process's own footprint (§4.1 measured, no typed number),
    which is what makes should_start() gate it thereafter. No gate here (calibration is what enables the gate)
    and no orders (a DiaryEngine has no order path). Returns the recorded footprint GB, or None on an unknown
    name. Run it in the engine's OWN process (§27b) so the footprint is that engine's, not a shared one."""
    eng = ENGINES.get(name)
    if eng is None:
        return None
    status = eng.work_fn()
    eng.diary_fn("✅ %s %s — %s [first calibration]" % (name, eng.clock_fn(), status))
    return diary_engine.record_footprint(name)


def _demo():
    import tempfile
    assert len(ENGINES) == 8, names()
    floor = 0.72
    # Isolate DIARY_DIR to an empty dir: "uncalibrated" must not depend on real footprint files a calibration
    # run may have left on disk (NB48 item 3).
    _saved = diary_engine.DIARY_DIR
    diary_engine.DIARY_DIR = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    try:
        for name, eng in ENGINES.items():
            # (1) no order path: a DiaryEngine has no place/order method — diary-only by construction
            assert not any(hasattr(eng, m) for m in ("place", "order", "buy", "sell")), name
            # (2) fail-closed while UNCALIBRATED (footprint None) — BUILT-not-LIVE
            assert eng.run_once(True, 9.9, floor)["started"] is False, "%s must not start uncalibrated" % name
    finally:
        diary_engine.DIARY_DIR = _saved
    # (3) calibrated + READY + RAM -> the diary line runs (proven with an injected footprint, no real run)
    e = ENGINES["Overnight"]
    posts = []
    e.diary_fn = posts.append
    e.footprint_gb = 0.2                                   # simulate a recorded footprint
    r = e.run_once(True, floor + 0.5)
    assert r["started"] and r["status"] == "ok" and "diary scaffold" in posts[-1] and "🙋" not in posts[-1]
    e.footprint_gb = None
    print("diary_engines demo OK: 8 §27-gated wrappers, diary-only, fail-closed until calibrated, quiet.")

> LEAD RULING R-PROVEN-BY-SE (origin: **LEAD-PROPOSED; OWNER-SAID "yes" 2026-09-23 04:40**, recorded before it ran per §5): every typed live-trade count is removed from docs and code. This repo had typed **"30 live trades to validate"** in `scripts/v8_engine_data.py` (4 countdowns + 4 PT rows) and `design/v8/Engine.dc.html`; both are now replaced with the promotion rule in words. **An engine's live record is "proven" when it beats flat by more than 2× its measured standard error** — the existing promotion rule. **This judges the RECORD only; it never gates a trade.** No numeric trade-count threshold governs anything. (`engine_page.countdown_to_verdict` was already un-typed — it counts accumulated live trades and reports depth, gating nothing — so it needed no change.)

> LEAD RULING R-ORIGIN-TAGS (origin: **OWNER-SAID 2026-09-23**): every ruling archived in `docs/dispatches/` carries an origin tag — **OWNER-SAID / LEAD-PROPOSED / BUILDER-PROPOSED**. Anything that changes a law, a threshold, or the owner's daily job needs an explicit owner **"yes" recorded in the archive before it runs**. A LEAD-PROPOSED or BUILDER-PROPOSED change to a law/threshold/daily-job that lacks a recorded owner "yes" does not run.

> LEAD RULING R-CLOCK (origin: **OWNER-SAID 2026-09-23**): every time in every report and every pause/deadline decision comes from the **machine clock** (`date` / `Get-Date`), read at the moment it is quoted — **never estimated or extrapolated**. A run states a time only after reading the clock; a pause decision (e.g. the 08:30 stop) re-reads the clock rather than projecting from an earlier read. Estimated times presented as fact are a self-flag. _Why: on 2026-09-23 a run reported "~08:10 IST" (extrapolated from a 02:52 start) when the real time was 05:14 IST — a ~3-hour error that also mis-based the pause decision._

"""NB40 item 3 — the twin-run REHEARSAL ROOT.

Redirect every LIVE store/ledger/notify path the order path writes, so a replay of a recorded day cannot touch a
live file. Built + tested here; the actual Tuesday replay runs after close in the isolated clone (twin_run).

Two independent layers, matching twin_run's design, so a miss in either still fails closed:
  PREVENTIVE — `redirect(root)` monkeypatches the known write-target module constants (paper.LEDGER,
    book20_runner.LEDGER, book20_runner.READOUT) to files UNDER `root`, and swaps notify.notify for a capture that
    posts nothing, for the duration of the replay; everything is restored on exit.
  DETECTIVE  — `no_live_writes(fn)` hashes every live ledger/store (twin_run's guarded set, which since NB38
    includes the MAIN paper ledger) before and after fn, and reports any path that changed. A change is the loud
    failure — so even a write path `redirect` forgot is caught.

def _mark(ok, step, detail=""):
    tag = "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"
    line = "%s %s — %s%s" % (tag, _ist().strftime("%H:%M:%S"), step, (" · " + detail) if detail else "")
    print(line)
    _lines.append((ok, line))
    return ok


def _step(step, fn):
    try:
        ok, detail = fn()
        return _mark(bool(ok), step, detail)
    except Exception as e:
        return _mark(False, step, "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:160]))

def _summary(tmp):
    from scripts import day_report
    day_report.summary()                               # reads the real ledger; notify dropped under --demo
    return True, "15:40 summary generated (log only)"


def run():
    from scripts.memory_guard import free_gb
    free_before = free_gb()
    from data_acquisition import kite
    from engine1_rebuild import gate, book20_runner as book20
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="monday_rehearsal_")

    _step("1. START_E1_KITE → catcher on 127.0.0.1 → token stored", lambda: _catcher_and_token(tmp, kite))
    _step("2. verify call", lambda: _verify(tmp, kite))
    _step("3. recorders start (chains, ticks, NSE/BSE, depth, positioning)", lambda: _recorders(tmp, kite))
    _step("4. book20 runner up, gate.order_ok per poll", lambda: _book20_poll_held(book20, gate))
    _step("5. KILL and RESUME", lambda: _kill_resume(tmp, gate))
    _step("6. breaker trip on a synthetic streak", lambda: _breaker(tmp))
    _step("7. trade cards written to log only", lambda: _trade_card_to_log(book20, gate))
    _step("8. site build and publish", lambda: _site(tmp))
    _step("9. 15:40 summary", lambda: _summary(tmp))
    _step("10. ROOF executor path (R-ROOF-EXECUTOR): desk book via the single order path", lambda: _roof_executor(gate))

    free_after = free_gb()
    ok = all(o for o, _ in _lines)
    hold_state = "HELD (R-HOLD-AND-FIX flag present, no AUDIT-3 pass)" if gate.held() else "NOT HELD"
    with open(OUT_MD, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("# Monday-morning rehearsal (R-MONDAY-REHEARSAL) — %s IST\n\n" % _ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"))
        f.write("_Fake token, temp environment, market closed. No Telegram left this run (demo context drops "
                "every send). The real token, KILL flag, breaker day-flag and order-hold were untouched._\n\n")
        f.write("Free RAM: **%s GB before → %s GB after**.\n\n" % (free_before, free_after))
        f.write("Order-hold state (unchanged by the rehearsal): **%s** — the state SMOKE-3E left.\n\n" % hold_state)
        f.write("```\n" + "\n".join(l for _, l in _lines) + "\n```\n")
        f.write("\n**RESULT: %s**\n" % ("ALL PASS" if ok else "FAIL — see the lines above"))
    print("\nfree RAM: %s GB before -> %s GB after | hold: %s" % (free_before, free_after, hold_state))
    print("RESULT:", "ALL PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    return ok


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--demo" in sys.argv or "--run" in sys.argv:
        sys.exit(0 if run() else 1)
    print("run with --demo (keeps every send off Telegram):  python -m scripts.monday_rehearsal --demo")

===== BEGIN CLAUDE-PRICE-ENGINE-ARCHITECTURE.md =====
# Claude Price Engine — architecture, end to end
Written 28 Sep 2026 by the lead, on the owner's instruction ("your domain now"). Origin tag: LEAD-DESIGNED, owner to accept.
Rule: every number below is either a formula, a measured value, or a data gate. No typed thresholds.
The [redacted] Price Engine uses THIS SAME architecture with one difference: its ruler shelf is the owner's 35 rulers, untouchable.

---

## 0. One-paragraph summary
A second price engine that searches the same way the [redacted] Price Engine does (exact-condition patterns, bands, luck court,
sealed holdout), but over a wider ruler shelf that includes forecast rulers, option-derived rulers, flows, market state,
microstructure and calendar. It decides direction (long / short), horizon (close-to-open, daily, intraday-15m) and vehicle
(cash long, futures short, index futures, defined-risk index options). It trades paper in its own two accounts (flat, adaptive)
and shares the desk's execution chain, gates, cards and site. Nothing in it touches the [redacted] Price Engine's rulers or code.

## 1. Scope and boundaries
- Universe: the 210 NSE F&O cash names + NIFTY and BANKNIFTY near/next-month futures; index options only after the option-chain
  data gate (§4) is met.
- Cadence classes (each with its own exam and its own patterns, never mixed): (a) daily — signal on the daily bar, entry next open;
  (b) intraday-15m — signal on a completed 15-minute bar, entry next bar's open; (c) close-to-open — signal at 15:25 on the
  index future, exit 09:20 next day (this absorbs the Overnight engine).
- Not in scope: words/news, fundamentals, real money.

## 2. Data it reads (all already on the shelf or recorded by the desk)
- 1-minute bars 2020-01 → present for the 210 (top-up daily), daily bars derived from them.
- Order-book depth (5 levels) per stock during market hours (the depth camera), spreads, trade intensity.
- Option chains for NIFTY/BANKNIFTY when the Insurance camera records them (data gate §4).
- Free public flows: delivery %, FII/DII net, participant-wise OI, bulk/block deals (where the archive allows; walled series
  accrue forward only).
- Macro rulers: India VIX, USD/INR, crude, US close, GIFT Nifty pre-open, 10-yr yield (free sources already wired).
- Calendar: earnings/results dates, dividends, index-change dates (Calendar engine's tables), expiry calendar.
- Data hygiene: split/bonus adjusted (already), point-in-time universe still ABSENT (survivorship badge stays on every result).

## 3. The ruler shelf (Claude's set)
Every ruler has: a family, a type (STATE = what is now; FORECAST = a model's guess about the next window), a window given as a
BAND (never one number), and the cadence classes it may be used in.

Family A — Price / trend (STATE): RSI, MACD line/histogram, Bollinger position (z of close vs band), ADX, VWAP distance,
distance to prior-day high/low, opening-range break, gap % vs prior close, distance to 52-week high/low, relative strength vs
Nifty, relative strength vs own sector, rolling beta, N-day return rank across the universe.
Family B — Volume (STATE): volume vs its own 20-day, OBV slope, delivery % vs own 14/21-day median (Dubai's lens, reused as a
ruler), turnover rank across the universe.
Family C — Volatility (STATE + FORECAST): ATR, realised vol (bands of windows), vol-of-vol, range compression/expansion ratio,
FORECAST: next-window vol from a HAR-style regression (daily/weekly/monthly realised vol → next day), used only after its own
forecast-vs-actual exam (§5.1).
Family D — Options-derived (STATE + FORECAST; index only until the gate opens): implied move to expiry, IV rank vs own history,
put/call OI ratio, skew (25-delta), max-pain distance, futures basis, days-to-expiry / expiry-week flag.
Family E — Flows / positioning (STATE): FII/DII net (bands of days), participant-wise OI change, retail positioning reading,
bulk/block deal flag (last N sessions).
Family F — Market state (STATE): VIX level and 5-day change, advance/decline ratio, % of universe above 50/200-day averages,
new highs minus new lows, USD/INR 5-day change, crude 5-day change, US close overnight return, GIFT pre-open gap.
Family G — Microstructure (STATE, intraday classes only): order-book imbalance (bid depth vs ask depth at 5 levels), spread in
bps, depth at touch vs own median, trade intensity (prints per minute vs own median), large-print flag, intraday extreme
reversal tendency (distance from day high/low late in the day), pre-open indicative vs prior close.
Family H — Calendar (STATE): day of week, time-of-day bucket, earnings within N sessions (bands), dividend proximity,
results-season flag, expiry-week flag.

Freeze rule: this shelf is versioned. Adding a ruler = new shelf version = every existing pattern keeps its record; new rulers
only enter new candidates. Rulers are never compressed or de-duplicated (owner law) — correlated rulers are handled by the
luck court, not by deletion.

## 4. Data gates (when a family may be used)
A family is usable in a cadence class only when its history covers enough sessions for the minimum-cases floor (§5.4) to be
reachable in TRAIN. Concretely: option-derived rulers wait until recorded chains cover ≥ the number of sessions the floor
formula needs (the desk computes and prints it); flows from walled archives use forward capture only; microstructure rulers
use recorded depth only (no reconstruction). Until a gate opens, its family is simply absent from the search — no
placeholders.

## 5. The search and the exam (same court as the [redacted] Price Engine)
5.1 FORECAST rulers first pass their own exam: forecast vs realised on the TRAIN/TEST split, scored against the naive forecast
    (yesterday's value); a forecast ruler enters the shelf only if it beats naive on TEST by more than its measured standard
    error. Failing forecasters are kept as STATE rulers (their inputs), not as forecasts.
5.2 Grammar: conditions = 1 or 2 rulers + optional regime (Family F state bucket), every part true on the same bar (exact
    event), thresholds as bands (quantile bands of the ruler's own history, never fixed levels), horizons as bands per cadence.
    Shapes: direct, inverse, lag (3–7 bars), trigger, stretch-and-snap, divergence (the registry; new shapes register).
5.3 Split: 70 / 20 / 10 chronological — TRAIN / TEST / sealed HOLDOUT (opened once per tier, never tunes). Walk-forward
    slices inside TRAIN→TEST; permutation mutants; block bootstrap for the band.
5.4 Scoring: line = the target's own base rate + margin m (costs + fade); Wilson lower bound must clear the line; minimum-cases
    floor n_min = the sample size at which the Wilson interval width falls below the pattern's measured edge (computed per
    pattern, printed, never typed); luck correction counts every candidate ever tried (online FDR). Rank by LB·W − (1−LB)·L − c.
5.5 Costs inside the exam: Zerodha 2026 table (fixed per-order charges + STT/GST/stamp/exchange as measured), plus the fill law:
    daily/close-to-open entries at the next open at the real open or worse; intraday entries at the touch ± recorded spread,
    walk-the-book beyond depth at touch. Certified R is scale-invariant; the size crossover vs fixed charges is reported per
    pattern (net edge at ₹1k / ₹5k / ₹10k risk) as information, not as a gate.
5.6 Certification: survivors of HOLDOUT with n ≥ n_min and positive margin, per cadence class. Everything else runs as a
    walk-forward diary (scored daily, no orders). Retired patterns stay retired; modified = new ID.

## 6. Direction and vehicle rules (decided, no open items)
- Long signal on a cash name → buy cash (delivery), next open.
- Short signal on a cash name → short via the single-stock future only (never cash), roll/exit before expiry week; futures costs
  and margin in the exam.
- Index pattern → near-month index future, one lot only if margin fits the account (SPAN check), else record-only.
- Options vehicle (index only, after the §4 gate): allowed when the pattern's expected move over its horizon is smaller than the
  implied move (a Family D ruler) → a defined-risk vertical spread in the signal's direction, one lot, max loss = the spread
  width; never naked. Exam on recorded chains only. Until certified on chains, the same pattern trades the future.

## 7. Trade management — end to end (applies to both price engines)
7.1 Pre-trade (every one must pass; fail = HELD with the reason logged):
    six execution checks (liquid universe, per-stock liquidity vs own median, size vs volume, impact model, slippage budget,
    time-of-day and price-band/halt), plus: event blackout (no entry if earnings/results within the pattern's horizon; dividend
    ex-date handled), regime filter (the pattern's own certified regime only), position limits (below), capital affordability,
    kill switch, breaker, exam-conform proof marker present.
7.2 Position sizing (a 0→1 multiplier on full Kelly, computed, never fixed):
    f_kelly = W_lb − (1 − W_lb)/R, where W_lb = Wilson lower bound of the pattern's win rate, R = avg win ÷ avg loss (measured);
    confidence c = 1 − (Wilson interval width ÷ W_lb) clipped to [0,1] (small samples → small c);
    f = f_kelly × c, then capped so that the size × the pattern's measured worst drawdown never exceeds the account's
    drawdown cap (cap = the account's own worst backtest drawdown); book throttle: f × (1 − current book drawdown ÷ cap);
    cluster cap: correlated open positions (same sector or rolling-correlation cluster) share one cap; regime scale from
    Circle Weighted when it is live (until then ×1). Size = 0 when net edge after costs ≤ 0.
    FLAT reference: 1R = a fixed fraction of the account (1R = ₹1,000 on ₹2,00,000), shares = 1R ÷ stop distance.
    Standing rule: a pattern trades FLAT until its live record reaches n_min; ADAPTIVE runs in shadow and takes over only when
    it beats FLAT by more than 2× its measured standard error.
7.3 Position limits: max open positions = floor(account ÷ (1R × the measured average stop multiple)) capped by the cluster
    rule; one position per name per engine; opposite signals in one name at one moment → recorded, not executed.
7.4 Entry: daily/close-to-open at the next open, filled at the real open or worse; intraday at the next bar, limit at touch,
    walk the book beyond touch depth; partial fills recorded.
7.5 Management (only certified variants per pattern; the exam decides which): fixed stop / fixed target (template);
    time exit at horizon end; trailing; break-even move; partial exits. Gap through a stop → exit at the open with the real cost.
    Daily mark-to-market at bid/ask, not last. Positions survive restarts (ledger is the truth; verified by the restart test).
7.6 Portfolio rails: per-engine breaker trips when the consecutive-loss streak exceeds the engine's own worst backtest streak
    (auto-resume next session); daily loss halt when the day's loss exceeds k × the book's measured daily standard deviation
    (k measured from the worst backtest day); KILL/RESUME by the owner; real-money rail closed.
7.7 Post-trade: costs charged, fill vs mid recorded (TCA ledger), practice counters, ledger row, PLACED/CLOSED cards, site update
    within a minute, roof copy into the desk book.
7.8 Feedback loop: every fire (taken or skipped) is scored; pattern stage ladder TEST→PAPER→LIVE by Wilson LB with counters reset
    per stage; live exit when the Wilson UPPER bound falls below the line over a rolling window; recency may shrink or suspend,
    never promote; monthly fill-law calibration from real fills; failures go to probation diaries.

## 8. Live chain and readiness (shared with the desk)
06:00 login → cameras start after the token with measured floors → 08:15 self-check line (cameras, gate, queue) → 09:15 orders
→ close-done line → evening evaluation on the day's bar → nightly full rehearsal of the whole morning on the real stack
(fake token, recorded quotes, measured memory at each step) → PASS/NOT READY before the owner taps. A desk day-log writes one
timestamped line per step. Market-hours mode: the laptop runs only the desk between 08:30 and the close-done line.

## 9. Build order (chunks; each chunk = backtest on the store first, then diary, then paper)
1. Ruler shelf v1 computed on the 1-min store for the 210 + index futures; sanity report (coverage, NaNs, ranges).
2. Forecast-ruler exam (§5.1): HAR vol forecast vs naive; implied-move only when chains exist.
3. Daily cadence search (families A, B, C, E, F, H) → exam → certified/diaries; report the size crossover per survivor.
4. Close-to-open cadence on index futures (absorbs Overnight) → exam.
5. Intraday-15m cadence with microstructure (family G) on recorded depth days → exam (small at first; grows with recording).
6. Options vehicle (family D) — waits for the chain data gate.
7. Paper accounts (flat + adaptive) opened after one independent smoke check; live only under the same chain and rails.
Timeline: unmeasured until chunk 1 runs and reports its rate.

## 10. Data dependencies (not questions — facts to check on the store before each chunk)
- Chunk 1–4 need only the 1-min store, daily bars, the free macro series and the calendar tables: available now.
- Delivery % history needs the daily bhavcopy archive (free); confirm depth of history on disk.
- Flows history from NSE archives is bot-walled: forward capture only unless the owner drops files.
- Chunk 5 needs recorded depth days: available from the first full recording day onward; grows daily.
- Chunk 6 needs recorded option chains: none yet.

## 11. What I would still test before trusting any survivor
- Sealed-holdout replay with the exact live code (twin run) for every certified pattern.
- Fill-law calibration against the first 50 real paper fills.
- The size crossover: survivors whose edge at ₹1k risk is below the fixed charges are certified but flagged
  "needs larger size" — they trade flat ₹1k in paper for the record, never as evidence of money.

[redacted] Price Engine: identical §§1–11 with the ruler shelf replaced by the owner's 35 rulers; the 35 are never edited, added to,
or compressed.

===== BEGIN CLAUDE-PRICE-ENGINE-ARCHITECTURE.md =====
# Claude Price Engine — architecture, end to end
Written 28 Sep 2026 by the lead, on the owner's instruction ("your domain now"). Origin tag: LEAD-DESIGNED, owner to accept.
Rule: every number below is either a formula, a measured value, or a data gate. No typed thresholds.
The [redacted] Price Engine uses THIS SAME architecture with one difference: its ruler shelf is the owner's 35 rulers, untouchable.

---

## 0. One-paragraph summary
A second price engine that searches the same way the [redacted] Price Engine does (exact-condition patterns, bands, luck court,
sealed holdout), but over a wider ruler shelf that includes forecast rulers, option-derived rulers, flows, market state,
microstructure and calendar. It decides direction (long / short), horizon (close-to-open, daily, intraday-15m) and vehicle
(cash long, futures short, index futures, defined-risk index options). It trades paper in its own two accounts (flat, adaptive)
and shares the desk's execution chain, gates, cards and site. Nothing in it touches the [redacted] Price Engine's rulers or code.

## 1. Scope and boundaries
- Universe: the 210 NSE F&O cash names + NIFTY and BANKNIFTY near/next-month futures; index options only after the option-chain
  data gate (§4) is met.
- Cadence classes (each with its own exam and its own patterns, never mixed): (a) daily — signal on the daily bar, entry next open;
  (b) intraday-15m — signal on a completed 15-minute bar, entry next bar's open; (c) close-to-open — signal at 15:25 on the
  index future, exit 09:20 next day (this absorbs the Overnight engine).
- Not in scope: words/news, fundamentals, real money.

## 2. Data it reads (all already on the shelf or recorded by the desk)
- 1-minute bars 2020-01 → present for the 210 (top-up daily), daily bars derived from them.
- Order-book depth (5 levels) per stock during market hours (the depth camera), spreads, trade intensity.
- Option chains for NIFTY/BANKNIFTY when the Insurance camera records them (data gate §4).
- Free public flows: delivery %, FII/DII net, participant-wise OI, bulk/block deals (where the archive allows; walled series
  accrue forward only).
- Macro rulers: India VIX, USD/INR, crude, US close, GIFT Nifty pre-open, 10-yr yield (free sources already wired).
- Calendar: earnings/results dates, dividends, index-change dates (Calendar engine's tables), expiry calendar.
- Data hygiene: split/bonus adjusted (already), point-in-time universe still ABSENT (survivorship badge stays on every result).

## 3. The ruler shelf (Claude's set)
Every ruler has: a family, a type (STATE = what is now; FORECAST = a model's guess about the next window), a window given as a
BAND (never one number), and the cadence classes it may be used in.

Family A — Price / trend (STATE): RSI, MACD line/histogram, Bollinger position (z of close vs band), ADX, VWAP distance,
distance to prior-day high/low, opening-range break, gap % vs prior close, distance to 52-week high/low, relative strength vs
Nifty, relative strength vs own sector, rolling beta, N-day return rank across the universe.
Family B — Volume (STATE): volume vs its own 20-day, OBV slope, delivery % vs own 14/21-day median (Dubai's lens, reused as a
ruler), turnover rank across the universe.
Family C — Volatility (STATE + FORECAST): ATR, realised vol (bands of windows), vol-of-vol, range compression/expansion ratio,
FORECAST: next-window vol from a HAR-style regression (daily/weekly/monthly realised vol → next day), used only after its own
forecast-vs-actual exam (§5.1).
Family D — Options-derived (STATE + FORECAST; index only until the gate opens): implied move to expiry, IV rank vs own history,
put/call OI ratio, skew (25-delta), max-pain distance, futures basis, days-to-expiry / expiry-week flag.
Family E — Flows / positioning (STATE): FII/DII net (bands of days), participant-wise OI change, retail positioning reading,
bulk/block deal flag (last N sessions).
Family F — Market state (STATE): VIX level and 5-day change, advance/decline ratio, % of universe above 50/200-day averages,
new highs minus new lows, USD/INR 5-day change, crude 5-day change, US close overnight return, GIFT pre-open gap.
Family G — Microstructure (STATE, intraday classes only): order-book imbalance (bid depth vs ask depth at 5 levels), spread in
bps, depth at touch vs own median, trade intensity (prints per minute vs own median), large-print flag, intraday extreme
reversal tendency (distance from day high/low late in the day), pre-open indicative vs prior close.
Family H — Calendar (STATE): day of week, time-of-day bucket, earnings within N sessions (bands), dividend proximity,
results-season flag, expiry-week flag.

Freeze rule: this shelf is versioned. Adding a ruler = new shelf version = every existing pattern keeps its record; new rulers
only enter new candidates. Rulers are never compressed or de-duplicated (owner law) — correlated rulers are handled by the
luck court, not by deletion.

## 4. Data gates (when a family may be used)
A family is usable in a cadence class only when its history covers enough sessions for the minimum-cases floor (§5.4) to be
reachable in TRAIN. Concretely: option-derived rulers wait until recorded chains cover ≥ the number of sessions the floor
formula needs (the desk computes and prints it); flows from walled archives use forward capture only; microstructure rulers
use recorded depth only (no reconstruction). Until a gate opens, its family is simply absent from the search — no
placeholders.

## 5. The search and the exam (same court as the [redacted] Price Engine)
5.1 FORECAST rulers first pass their own exam: forecast vs realised on the TRAIN/TEST split, scored against the naive forecast
    (yesterday's value); a forecast ruler enters the shelf only if it beats naive on TEST by more than its measured standard
    error. Failing forecasters are kept as STATE rulers (their inputs), not as forecasts.
5.2 Grammar: conditions = 1 or 2 rulers + optional regime (Family F state bucket), every part true on the same bar (exact
    event), thresholds as bands (quantile bands of the ruler's own history, never fixed levels), horizons as bands per cadence.
    Shapes: direct, inverse, lag (3–7 bars), trigger, stretch-and-snap, divergence (the registry; new shapes register).
5.3 Split: 70 / 20 / 10 chronological — TRAIN / TEST / sealed HOLDOUT (opened once per tier, never tunes). Walk-forward
    slices inside TRAIN→TEST; permutation mutants; block bootstrap for the band.
5.4 Scoring: line = the target's own base rate + margin m (costs + fade); Wilson lower bound must clear the line; minimum-cases
    floor n_min = the sample size at which the Wilson interval width falls below the pattern's measured edge (computed per
    pattern, printed, never typed); luck correction counts every candidate ever tried (online FDR). Rank by LB·W − (1−LB)·L − c.
5.5 Costs inside the exam: Zerodha 2026 table (fixed per-order charges + STT/GST/stamp/exchange as measured), plus the fill law:
    daily/close-to-open entries at the next open at the real open or worse; intraday entries at the touch ± recorded spread,
    walk-the-book beyond depth at touch. Certified R is scale-invariant; the size crossover vs fixed charges is reported per
    pattern (net edge at ₹1k / ₹5k / ₹10k risk) as information, not as a gate.
5.6 Certification: survivors of HOLDOUT with n ≥ n_min and positive margin, per cadence class. Everything else runs as a
    walk-forward diary (scored daily, no orders). Retired patterns stay retired; modified = new ID.

## 6. Direction and vehicle rules (decided, no open items)
- Long signal on a cash name → buy cash (delivery), next open.
- Short signal on a cash name → short via the single-stock future only (never cash), roll/exit before expiry week; futures costs
  and margin in the exam.
- Index pattern → near-month index future, one lot only if margin fits the account (SPAN check), else record-only.
- Options vehicle (index only, after the §4 gate): allowed when the pattern's expected move over its horizon is smaller than the
  implied move (a Family D ruler) → a defined-risk vertical spread in the signal's direction, one lot, max loss = the spread
  width; never naked. Exam on recorded chains only. Until certified on chains, the same pattern trades the future.

## 7. Trade management — end to end (applies to both price engines)
7.1 Pre-trade (every one must pass; fail = HELD with the reason logged):
    six execution checks (liquid universe, per-stock liquidity vs own median, size vs volume, impact model, slippage budget,
    time-of-day and price-band/halt), plus: event blackout (no entry if earnings/results within the pattern's horizon; dividend
    ex-date handled), regime filter (the pattern's own certified regime only), position limits (below), capital affordability,
    kill switch, breaker, exam-conform proof marker present.
7.2 Position sizing (a 0→1 multiplier on full Kelly, computed, never fixed):
    f_kelly = W_lb − (1 − W_lb)/R, where W_lb = Wilson lower bound of the pattern's win rate, R = avg win ÷ avg loss (measured);
    confidence c = 1 − (Wilson interval width ÷ W_lb) clipped to [0,1] (small samples → small c);
    f = f_kelly × c, then capped so that the size × the pattern's measured worst drawdown never exceeds the account's
    drawdown cap (cap = the account's own worst backtest drawdown); book throttle: f × (1 − current book drawdown ÷ cap);
    cluster cap: correlated open positions (same sector or rolling-correlation cluster) share one cap; regime scale from
    Circle Weighted when it is live (until then ×1). Size = 0 when net edge after costs ≤ 0.
    FLAT reference: 1R = a fixed fraction of the account (1R = ₹1,000 on ₹2,00,000), shares = 1R ÷ stop distance.
    Standing rule: a pattern trades FLAT until its live record reaches n_min; ADAPTIVE runs in shadow and takes over only when
    it beats FLAT by more than 2× its measured standard error.
7.3 Position limits: max open positions = floor(account ÷ (1R × the measured average stop multiple)) capped by the cluster
    rule; one position per name per engine; opposite signals in one name at one moment → recorded, not executed.
7.4 Entry: daily/close-to-open at the next open, filled at the real open or worse; intraday at the next bar, limit at touch,
    walk the book beyond touch depth; partial fills recorded.
7.5 Management (only certified variants per pattern; the exam decides which): fixed stop / fixed target (template);
    time exit at horizon end; trailing; break-even move; partial exits. Gap through a stop → exit at the open with the real cost.
    Daily mark-to-market at bid/ask, not last. Positions survive restarts (ledger is the truth; verified by the restart test).
7.6 Portfolio rails: per-engine breaker trips when the consecutive-loss streak exceeds the engine's own worst backtest streak
    (auto-resume next session); daily loss halt when the day's loss exceeds k × the book's measured daily standard deviation
    (k measured from the worst backtest day); KILL/RESUME by the owner; real-money rail closed.
7.7 Post-trade: costs charged, fill vs mid recorded (TCA ledger), practice counters, ledger row, PLACED/CLOSED cards, site update
    within a minute, roof copy into the desk book.
7.8 Feedback loop: every fire (taken or skipped) is scored; pattern stage ladder TEST→PAPER→LIVE by Wilson LB with counters reset
    per stage; live exit when the Wilson UPPER bound falls below the line over a rolling window; recency may shrink or suspend,
    never promote; monthly fill-law calibration from real fills; failures go to probation diaries.

## 8. Live chain and readiness (shared with the desk)
06:00 login → cameras start after the token with measured floors → 08:15 self-check line (cameras, gate, queue) → 09:15 orders
→ close-done line → evening evaluation on the day's bar → nightly full rehearsal of the whole morning on the real stack
(fake token, recorded quotes, measured memory at each step) → PASS/NOT READY before the owner taps. A desk day-log writes one
timestamped line per step. Market-hours mode: the laptop runs only the desk between 08:30 and the close-done line.

## 9. Build order (chunks; each chunk = backtest on the store first, then diary, then paper)
1. Ruler shelf v1 computed on the 1-min store for the 210 + index futures; sanity report (coverage, NaNs, ranges).
2. Forecast-ruler exam (§5.1): HAR vol forecast vs naive; implied-move only when chains exist.
3. Daily cadence search (families A, B, C, E, F, H) → exam → certified/diaries; report the size crossover per survivor.
4. Close-to-open cadence on index futures (absorbs Overnight) → exam.
5. Intraday-15m cadence with microstructure (family G) on recorded depth days → exam (small at first; grows with recording).
6. Options vehicle (family D) — waits for the chain data gate.
7. Paper accounts (flat + adaptive) opened after one independent smoke check; live only under the same chain and rails.
Timeline: unmeasured until chunk 1 runs and reports its rate.

## 10. Data dependencies (not questions — facts to check on the store before each chunk)
- Chunk 1–4 need only the 1-min store, daily bars, the free macro series and the calendar tables: available now.
- Delivery % history needs the daily bhavcopy archive (free); confirm depth of history on disk.
- Flows history from NSE archives is bot-walled: forward capture only unless the owner drops files.
- Chunk 5 needs recorded depth days: available from the first full recording day onward; grows daily.
- Chunk 6 needs recorded option chains: none yet.

## 11. What I would still test before trusting any survivor
- Sealed-holdout replay with the exact live code (twin run) for every certified pattern.
- Fill-law calibration against the first 50 real paper fills.
- The size crossover: survivors whose edge at ₹1k risk is below the fixed charges are certified but flagged
  "needs larger size" — they trade flat ₹1k in paper for the record, never as evidence of money.

[redacted] Price Engine: identical §§1–11 with the ruler shelf replaced by the owner's 35 rulers; the 35 are never edited, added to,
or compressed.

HALT = os.environ.get("E1_HALT_FILE") or os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "HALT")


def _load(p):
    try:
        return json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _building():
    try:
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import desk_clock
        return desk_clock.building()
    except Exception:
        from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
        now = datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))
        m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
        return m >= 15 * 60 + 45 or m < 8 * 60 + 30      # fail toward the build window (keep going)


def should_continue():
    """Continue while the checklist has work — and, per R-BACKLOG, when it empties, pull the next backlog item
    into the checklist and keep going. Stop only for a HALT, a LEAD RULING NEEDED flag, the pause window, or an
    empty checklist AND empty backlog."""
    st = _load(STATE)
    flag = (st.get("flag") or "").strip()
    if os.path.exists(HALT) or flag != "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD" or not _building():
        return False
    remaining = [x for x in (_load(CHECKLIST).get("remaining") or []) if x]
    if remaining:
        return True
    try:                                              # checklist empty -> pull the next backlog item (R-BACKLOG)
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import backlog
        return bool(backlog.pull_next())
    except Exception:
        return False

tool_result: def floor(p0: float, n: int) -> float:
    """The minimum-effect floor, recomputed from the data (R-E1-FLOOR-FORMULA). No typed constant."""
    if n <= 0 or p0 <= 0 or p0 >= 1:
        return 0.0
    return _Z * math.sqrt(p0 * (1 - p0) / n)

def test_slice_of(bars: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    te, ho = split_indices(len(bars))
    return bars.iloc[te:ho]

**Engine 6, "ML-direct."** Written 2026-07-31 as QUEUE item 26 (promoted from `ROADMAP.md` A6) under
the architecture-first decree, CLAUDE.md §13.

_This is the shortest document in the architecture series, and deliberately so. E6 is the **last**
engine by owner decree, and almost everything about how it validates, sizes, nurses and demotes is
**inherited unchanged** from machinery that already has one home elsewhere. Writing those mechanisms
out again would create a second copy of the truth. What this document must contain is exactly the
three things the roadmap entry names: **the build-last decree written as arithmetic**, **the zero-
exemption rule made checkable**, and **what "the gauntlet has proven itself" means as measurable
preconditions** — because until now that phrase has been a feeling, and a feeling cannot gate an
engine. Every defense below states **metric · on-disk data source · numeric threshold · action**.
Risk is in **R** throughout; no rupee figure appears anywhere._

**The paper source for E6 is `ENGINE6_DECISIONS.md`** — a decision ledger, not a spec. That ledger
already holds the philosophy (no generator is an oracle; method selection is a counted search;
readable generators first), the generator catalog, the authority inversion, and six open gaps. This
document does not repeat it and does not replace it. It writes the **gate** the ledger implies.

fill, tagged with the engine, and:

  • holds N× when N engines make the SAME trade (same instrument + side) in the same window (clause 1);
